# s069 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. P1: Binding truth becomes inspectable
- Semantic invariant: What is real acquires force by being fixed, owed, demonstrated, and finally exposed to view.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:1-3 names the inevitable reality, while 69:7-8 and 69:18 command sight and public exposure.
- Surprising reach: The same lexical field materializes truth as an owned claim, a protected obligation, a fitted joint, a penetrating thrust, and a textile whose weave can be tested.

#### Subchannel A. Adjudicated reality and enforceable claim
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A disputed proposition is stabilized as fact, obligation, entitlement, and demonstrated judgment.
- Active motifs: ثبات مطابق للواقع ضد الباطل (`quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`); لزوم واجب واستحقاق ثابت (`quranic:root_000347:B002/m01`); حق مخصوص يملكه صاحبه (`quranic:root_000347:B003/m01`); محاقة يدعي كل طرف فيها الحق (`quranic:root_000347:B004/m01`); إثبات الحق وإظهاره (`quranic:root_000347:B005/m01`); حقيقة يلزم حفظها (`quranic:root_000347:B007/m01`); الدراية والعلم (`quranic:root_000473:B001/m01`); ردع يقرع السامع (`quranic:root_001219:B006/m01`); خلاف الصدق (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); نسبة الشيء أو صاحبه إلى الكذب (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `د ر ي`: 69:3 (أَدْرَىٰ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ)
- Synthesis: The fixed reality opposes falsehood, then becomes something binding, owed, privately held, contested, defended, and proved. Knowledge supplies the cognitive act, while rebuke can turn the hearer back toward the right and denial supplies the adversarial speech that makes demonstration necessary.

#### Subchannel B. Display, inspection, and removal of concealment
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Observers face a scene, inspect its visible marks, and have what was covered brought out.
- Active motifs: رؤية العين والبصيرة (`quranic:root_000531:B001/m01`); رأي القلب والتفكر (`quranic:root_000531:B002/m01`); الرؤيا في المنام (`quranic:root_000531:B003/m01`); تراء وتواجه (`quranic:root_000531:B004/m01`); مرأى ومنظر ومرآة (`quranic:root_000531:B006/m01`); إراءة وإظهار (`quranic:root_000531:B012/m01`); أرأيتك للتنبيه والاستخبار (`quranic:root_000531:B013/m01`); الستر والخفاء (`quranic:root_000428:B001/m01`); إزالة الخفاء والإظهار (`quranic:root_000428:B003/m01`); لمع البرق الخفي (`quranic:root_000428:B004/m01`); عرض الشيء وإبرازه للنظر (`quranic:root_001001:B002/m01`); العارض البادي من جهة (`quranic:root_001001:B003/m01`); معاريض الكلام وجه غير مصرح به (`quranic:root_001001:B010/m01`); الدراية والعلم (`quranic:root_000473:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `خ ف ي`: 69:18 (تَخْفَىٰ/خَافِيَةٌ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ); `د ر ي`: 69:3 (أَدْرَىٰ)
- Synthesis: Eye, inward judgment, dream, mutual facing, mirror-image, induced showing, and the interrogative call to report all organize modes of inspection. Concealment is removed rather than merely denied: a faint flash emerges from cloud, an object is displayed, and even indirect wording has an outer and inner face to be read.

#### Subchannel C. Exact fit, verified fabric, and straight penetration
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Parts prove sound by meeting their socket, holding a firm weave, or traveling straight through rather than glancing aside.
- Active motifs: طعنة استقامت حتى نفذت (`quranic:root_000347:B009/m01`); إحكام رصين في نسج أو كلام (`quranic:root_000347:B010/m01`); حق يطابق موضعه كالمفصل والوعاء (`quranic:root_000347:B011/m01`); أجزاء موصولة يقابل بعضها بعضا (`quranic:root_001198:B010/m01`); إحكام النسج (`quranic:root_000217:B007/m01`); التحام العظم بعد الكسر (`quranic:root_001664:B003/m01`); القوة وشدة الوطء (`quranic:root_000482:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً)
- Synthesis: Truth is given a mechanical test: the thrust runs straight and penetrates, speech or cloth is tightly made, and a joint or vessel matches its proper seat. Joined straps, sound weaving, and a knitted bone extend this exactness into assembly and repair, while forceful footing tests whether the construction holds.

### 2. P1: Defiance meets coercive mastery
- Semantic invariant: Exceeded limits, refused obedience, and imposed service are answered by a force that masters agents and elements.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:4-7 and 69:9-11 pair denial and rebellion with overpowering punishment, and 69:7 says the wind was subjected over the people.
- Surprising reach: Transgression shifts between moral rebellion, physical overflow, tyrannical rule, forced labor, and the smooth elevated rock on which resistance finds no purchase.

#### Subchannel A. Limit-breaking rebellion and tyrannical command
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Agents leave obedience, harden into domination, and organize others around a head of error.
- Active motifs: مجاوزة الحد في العصيان (`quranic:root_000936:B001/m01`); الطاغوت رأس الضلالة والطغيان (`quranic:root_000936:B003/m01`); الطاغية المتجبر (`quranic:root_000936:B004/m01`); مجاوزة الحد في العصيان (`quranic:root_000937:B001/m01`); الطاغوت رأس الضلالة (`quranic:root_000937:B003/m01`); العُتُوّ عن الطاعة (`quranic:root_000981:B001/m01`); الخروج عن الطاعة (`quranic:root_001022:B001/m01`); تسخير العامل بلا أجرة (`quranic:root_000685:B002/m01`); عزم ونهوض إلى الأمر (`quranic:root_001273:B003/m01`); رعاية وحفظ وولاية (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); عسر ومنع (`quranic:root_000217:B009/m01`); حمل على أمر (`quranic:root_000217:B010/m01`); كذب عليك بمعنى الزم وعليك به (`quranic:root_001290:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ط غ ي`: 69:5 (طَّاغِيَةِ), 69:11 (طَغَا); `ع ت و`: 69:6 (عَاتِيَةٍ); `ع ص ي`: 69:10 (عَصَ); `س خ ر`: 69:7 (سَخَّرَ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ)
- Synthesis: Moral excess becomes a hierarchy: rebellion crosses a limit, a tyrant or false cultic head directs it, and pride refuses command. Forced labor, obstructive difficulty, compulsion, and an imperative of obligation supply the coercive underside, while resolve and governance show the same capacity turned toward taking charge of an undertaking.

#### Subchannel B. Overflowing force as dominant punishment
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Water, wind, cry, or penalty rises beyond measure and overwhelms what lies before it.
- Active motifs: طغيان الماء وما يجري مجراه (`quranic:root_000936:B002/m01`); الطاغية عقوبة غالبة (`quranic:root_000936:B005/m01`); علو الماء والقوة الجارفة (`quranic:root_000937:B002/m01`); زيادة وعلو (`quranic:root_000537:B001/m01`); الريح والنسيم وهبوب الهواء (`quranic:root_000609:B003/m01`); الريح قوة وغلبة (`quranic:root_000609:B011/m01`); ريح صرصر باردة (`quranic:root_000857:B004/m01`); الواقعة النازلة الشديدة (`quranic:root_001675:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ط غ ي`: 69:5 (طَّاغِيَةِ), 69:11 (طَغَا); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)
- Synthesis: The moral image of excess is made physical in flood, gale, and a punishment whose own force crosses the limit. Rising and dominance are one operation here: water swells, wind gains the upper hand, cold intensifies it, and the event lands as a severe visitation.

#### Subchannel C. Subjection, service, and derisive reversal
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A power bends an instrument or worker to purpose, while mockery threatens to reverse who controls whom.
- Active motifs: تذليل الشيء وسوقه للغرض (`quranic:root_000685:B001/m01`); تسخير العامل بلا أجرة (`quranic:root_000685:B002/m01`); الاستهزاء والضحك من الغير (`quranic:root_000685:B003/m01`); المِلْك والتصرف (`quranic:root_001444:B002/m01`); المُلك والسلطان (`quranic:root_001444:B003/m01`); المتقدم القائد في الحيوان (`quranic:root_001444:B008/m01`); علو القهر والغلبة (`quranic:root_001188:B003/m01`); الأخذ بالسيرة والشكل (`quranic:root_000018:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س خ ر`: 69:7 (سَخَّرَ); `م ل ك`: 69:17 (مَلَكُ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً)
- Synthesis: Subjection ranges from making wind or vessel tractable to compelling unpaid labor and governing subjects. The leading animal and the sovereign model directed order; derision destabilizes that order by making a person the object of another's control, and imitation of a group's way shows mastery reproduced as conduct.

### 3. P1: Impact becomes capture and defeat
- Semantic invariant: A blow is followed through its full sequence: collision, opposed force, gripping control, collapse, and exhausted aftermath.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:4 names the striking calamity, 69:5-10 narrates destruction and seizure, and 69:14-16 extends impact into cosmic collapse.
- Surprising reach: The sequence reaches from knocking at a door and the clang of iron to wrestling holds, battlefield lines, uprooted bodies, and a road that consumes its traveler.

#### Subchannel A. Blow, clang, and battlefield collision
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Hard surfaces meet, opponents close, and the impact is heard and registered as a battle-event.
- Active motifs: ضرب شيء على شيء (`quranic:root_001219:B001/m01`); مقارعة ومضاربة (`quranic:root_001219:B002/m01`); صرصرة الصوت (`quranic:root_000857:B002/m01`); أثر الضرب وصوت الوقوع (`quranic:root_001675:B004/m01`); وقعة الحرب والإيقاع بالقوم (`quranic:root_001675:B005/m01`); مقاومة ومنازلة (`quranic:root_001273:B014/m01`); أخذة المصارعة (`quranic:root_000018:B011/m01`); صدق الحملة أو كذبها (`quranic:root_001290:B004/m01`); القوة وشدة الوطء (`quranic:root_000482:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً)
- Synthesis: Knocking, heroic encounter, the report of a hard fall, and the shock of battle form one collision scene. Wrestling grips and mutual resistance convert sound into control, while the truth or failure of a charge is tested by whether it carries through to thrust and strike.

#### Subchannel B. Capture, handle, and arresting grip
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A target is taken, held by an accessible grip, and prevented from escaping.
- Active motifs: حوز الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`); القبض والأسر (`quranic:root_000018:B003/m01`); رقية تمسك وتحبس (`quranic:root_000018:B004/m01`); مقبض الشيء المأخوذ به (`quranic:root_000018:B012/m01`); المدرى والحد المحدد (`quranic:root_000473:B004/m01`); قراع صلب ممسوح (`quranic:root_001219:B011/m01`); آلة قائمة وجزء قائم (`quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`); عود من خشب وطيب وآلة (`quranic:root_001058:B010/m01`); طاقة على المقابلة (`quranic:root_001198:B013/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `د ر ي`: 69:3 (أَدْرَىٰ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ)
- Synthesis: Taking becomes technically concrete through arrest, a charm that holds, a handle, a pointed implement, a hard stripped surface, a standing machine-part, and a wooden striking tool. The target's lack of capacity to face the force closes the mechanism: there is a grip and no adequate counter-grip.

#### Subchannel C. Rout, bodily fall, and spent capacity
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The defeated body is thrown down, loses motion and power, and remains as the trace of overwhelming pursuit.
- Active motifs: الطرح والسقوط إلى الأرض (`quranic:root_000859:B001/m01`); العلة التي تصرع صاحبها (`quranic:root_000859:B002/m01`); السقوط إلى الهلاك والفناء (`quranic:root_001596:B001/m01`); إلقاء النفس في التهلكة (`quranic:root_001596:B002/m01`); استهلاك الجهد واستفراغه (`quranic:root_001596:B009/m01`); التهلك في المفازة تحيرا (`quranic:root_001596:B010/m01`); وادي تهلّك في الباطل (`quranic:root_001596:B011/m01`); ضعف وقصور عن الفعل (`quranic:root_000985:B001/m01`); فوت وسبق وإفلات من الطلب (`quranic:root_000985:B002/m01`); جمود ووقوف وكلال (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`); عين قائمة ذاهبة البصر (`quranic:root_001273:B021/m01`); الموت بلفظ الإراحة (`quranic:root_000609:B016/m01`); أثر الدبر والركوب (`quranic:root_001675:B010/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ص ر ع`: 69:7 (صَرْعَىٰ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ); `ع ج ز`: 69:7 (أَعْجَازُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)
- Synthesis: Being felled joins ordinary defeat, seizure-like collapse, annihilation, self-endangerment, exhausted effort, bewildered circling, and falling into nullity. Inability and failed escape leave movement stalled; sight can remain physically intact yet functionally gone, death becomes an 'easing,' and the surviving body bears the abrasion of the ride.

### 4. P1: Air carries life, cold, and summons
- Semantic invariant: Moving air can animate, overpower, cool, voice, inflate, or leave a body at the moment of death.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:6-7 presents the lethal wind, and 69:13 turns blown air into the single trumpet blast.
- Surprising reach: One medium joins meteorology and physiology: gale, scent, lung, panting, hiccup, roar, and the final departing breath.

#### Subchannel A. Cold gale, contrary currents, and lost force
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Air moves as a severe current whose direction, temperature, and dominance determine the scene.
- Active motifs: الريح والنسيم وهبوب الهواء (`quranic:root_000609:B003/m01`); الرائحة والشم والطيب والنتن (`quranic:root_000609:B004/m01`); الريح قوة وغلبة (`quranic:root_000609:B011/m01`); صرصرة الصوت (`quranic:root_000857:B002/m01`); ريح صرصر باردة (`quranic:root_000857:B004/m01`); رياح مؤتفكات مختلفة المهاب (`quranic:root_000041:B004/m01`); ريح تقابل الدبور (`quranic:root_001198:B012/m01`); طغيان الماء وما يجري مجراه (`quranic:root_000936:B002/m01`); علو الماء والقوة الجارفة (`quranic:root_000937:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `ء ف ك`: 69:9 (مُؤْتَفِكَٰتُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ط غ ي`: 69:5 (طَّاغِيَةِ), 69:11 (طَغَا)
- Synthesis: The wind is not an abstract weather label: it blows, smells, roars, chills, changes direction, meets a contrary current, and can become the very figure of superior force. Flood and gale share the operation of rising until ordinary orientation and resistance fail.

#### Subchannel B. Breath, lung, inflation, and final exhalation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Air enters and leaves living bodies, expands tissue, strains respiration, and marks the loss of life.
- Active motifs: النفس والروح التي بها حياة البدن (`quranic:root_000609:B001/m01`); الراحة والاستراحة والإراحة (`quranic:root_000609:B007/m01`); الموت بلفظ الإراحة (`quranic:root_000609:B016/m01`); الرئة وما يصيبها (`quranic:root_000531:B009/m01`); إرسال الريح في الشيء (`quranic:root_001529:B001/m01`); انتفاخ الشيء وامتلاؤه بالريح (`quranic:root_001529:B002/m01`); انتفاخ الفخر والكبر (`quranic:root_001529:B006/m01`); تصعد النفس وانتفاخه (`quranic:root_000537:B004/m01`); فواق الصدر والنزع (`quranic:root_001188:B010/m01`); عرض الإنسان حماه وبدنه وظاهر وجهه (`quranic:root_001001:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ)
- Synthesis: Spirit, lung, inhaled or expelled air, swelling, proud inflation, panting, hiccup, and the death-rattle occupy one respiratory mechanism. Rest is the recovery of breath, while death is its terminal release; even social pride is materialized as a body puffed beyond its proper measure.

#### Subchannel C. Blast, roar, and receptive ear
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A forceful emission becomes signal, and a listening organ must receive and register it.
- Active motifs: إرسال الريح في الشيء (`quranic:root_001529:B001/m01`); قرن النفخ (`quranic:root_000891:B004/m01`); صرصرة الصوت (`quranic:root_000857:B002/m01`); الخَوَاة صوتا (`quranic:root_000450:B007/m01`); ارتفاع الصوت والجلبة (`quranic:root_001664:B005/m01`); الأذن الجارحة وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000022:B001/m01`); الإصغاء وقبول المسموع (`quranic:root_000022:B002/m01`); العلم والإعلام بالنداء (`quranic:root_000022:B003/m01`); طغي القوم صوتهم (`quranic:root_000936:B008/m01`); شقشقة البعير والخطيب (`quranic:root_000807:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `ء ذ ن`: 69:12 (أُذُنٌ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ)
- Synthesis: Blowing into the horn turns breath into a public acoustic event. Roar, clamor, collective voice, and the camel's or orator's protruding throat-sound converge on an ear that is both a physical receiver and an act of obedient attention; the call becomes knowledge when announced and heard. The `ط غ و` voice motif has no available surface-root anchor.

#### Subchannel D. Relief, alternation, and the return of motion
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: After strain, breath and activity return by rest, alternating labor, or an evening homecoming.
- Active motifs: الرواح والعشي ورد الماشية (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`); الراحة والاستراحة والإراحة (`quranic:root_000609:B007/m01`); المراوحة والتناوب (`quranic:root_000609:B008/m01`); السعة والانبساط الحسي (`quranic:root_000609:B009/m01`); الأريحية والنشاط للمعروف (`quranic:root_000609:B010/m01`); أوبة الوعي والقوة والراحة (`quranic:root_001188:B005/m01`); رجوع اللبن بين الحلبتين (`quranic:root_001188:B006/m01`); أخذ الشيء جرعات أو دفعات (`quranic:root_001188:B007/m01`); رجوع بعد انصراف وتثنية بعد بدء (`quranic:root_001058:B001/m01`); عادة ودرَبة ومواظبة (`quranic:root_001058:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ)
- Synthesis: Evening return, rest, alternating work, bodily expansion, and renewed generosity describe recovery as regained room to act. Awakening after illness and the returning milk between milkings give the same rhythm a physiological and pastoral form, while recurrence becomes habit through repeated performance.

### 5. P1: Vertical forms are hollowed, split, and leveled
- Semantic invariant: Tall bodies, palms, buildings, mountains, and sky lose their interior support and vertical distinction.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:7 compares the fallen people to hollow palm trunks, 69:14 levels earth and mountains, and 69:16 splits and weakens the sky.
- Surprising reach: Ruin is rendered through carpentry and anatomy: empty belly, uprooted trunk, loosened wall, torn skin, buried well, and sand ridge all undergo related structural failure.

#### Subchannel A. Hollow trunk, uprooting, and emptied interior
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A standing organic form loses root, contents, and usable strength before falling.
- Active motifs: الخلو والفراغ (`quranic:root_000450:B001/m01`); السقوط والانهدام (`quranic:root_000450:B002/m01`); التجافي والتخوية (`quranic:root_000450:B004/m01`); خواء الجوف (`quranic:root_000450:B005/m01`); مؤخر الشيء وعقبه (`quranic:root_000985:B004/m01`); رملة أو حبل مرتفع يشبه العجيزة (`quranic:root_000985:B005/m01`); شجرة النخل (`quranic:root_001483:B001/m01`); غصن ساقط أو يابس يتخذ منه (`quranic:root_000859:B007/m01`); قطع حاسم (`quranic:root_000322:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ); `ع ج ز`: 69:7 (أَعْجَازُ); `ن خ ل`: 69:7 (نَخْلٍ); `ص ر ع`: 69:7 (صَرْعَىٰ); `ح س م`: 69:7 (حُسُومًا)
- Synthesis: Vacancy, collapse, a gap between surfaces, and an empty belly converge on the palm-trunk image. The rear or lower part and a raised sand form preserve its contour, while fallen dry wood and decisive cutting explain how vertical continuity is lost; lack of inner reason or value supplies the human analogue.

#### Subchannel B. Wall, roof, skin, and vessel rupture
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A containing surface loosens, tears, or opens until what it shelters can no longer be held.
- Active motifs: انصداع الشيء وانفتاحه (`quranic:root_000807:B001/m01`); النصف والشق المقابل (`quranic:root_000807:B002/m01`); قطعة منشقة وثوب (`quranic:root_000807:B006/m01`); استرخاء وضعف في الشيء (`quranic:root_001688:B001/m01`); شق وخرق في أديم أو ثوب أو سقاء (`quranic:root_001688:B002/m01`); إيهاء اليد بكسر أو إصابة (`quranic:root_001688:B004/m01`); السقف والمظلّة المرفوعة (`quranic:root_001000:B002/m01`); قوام الأمر والعز إذا ثبت أو ثل (`quranic:root_001000:B006/m01`); الدق والهدم حتى التسوية (`quranic:root_000482:B001/m01`); إهالة التراب والدفن (`quranic:root_000482:B003/m01`); الأَرَضَة آكلة الخشب (`quranic:root_000025:B010/m01`); فساد القرحة بالمدة (`quranic:root_000025:B011/m01`); امتلاء الجرح بالمدة (`quranic:root_001664:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ); `و ه ي`: 69:16 (وَاهِيَةٌ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً); `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ)
- Synthesis: Crack, split side, detached piece, loose wall, unpatchable skin, and broken hand describe one failure of enclosure. Roof and sovereignty can both be 'thrown down'; demolition and burial fill the breach from above, while worm-eaten wood, suppurating tissue, and a pus-filled wound show slower versions of the same loss of integrity.

#### Subchannel C. Mountain mass reduced to low ground
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Raised, hard terrain is struck, spread, crowded, and brought level with the ground.
- Active motifs: السفل المقابل للسماء (`quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`); لزوم الأرض والتثاقل إليها (`quranic:root_000025:B006/m01`); الأَرْض الرعدة (`quranic:root_000025:B008/m01`); تجمع مرتفع صلب (`quranic:root_000217:B001/m01`); صلابة توقف الحفر (`quranic:root_000217:B005/m01`); دخول الجبال (`quranic:root_000217:B006/m01`); مصادفة رمل عريض (`quranic:root_000217:B011/m01`); الدق والهدم حتى التسوية (`quranic:root_000482:B001/m01`); الانبساط والانخفاض (`quranic:root_000482:B002/m01`); التزاحم والتراكم (`quranic:root_000482:B008/m01`); رملة أو حبل مرتفع يشبه العجيزة (`quranic:root_000985:B005/m01`); فرجة بين الرمال ونباتها (`quranic:root_000807:B007/m01`); أرض مرتفعة (`quranic:root_000537:B002/m01`); المكان المرتفع والسحاب الواقع (`quranic:root_001675:B016/m01`); الطغية الصفاة أو الموضع المرتفع (`quranic:root_000936:B006/m01`); الطغية نبذة من الشيء (`quranic:root_000936:B007/m01`); الطغية الصفاة الملساء (`quranic:root_000937:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً); `ع ج ز`: 69:7 (أَعْجَازُ); `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ط غ ي`: 69:5 (طَّاغِيَةِ), 69:11 (طَغَا)
- Synthesis: Earth is the lower plane against which mountain, broad sand ridge, elevated patch, lesser height, smooth high rock, and detached fragment are measured. Hardness first stops the digger; then pounding, spreading, trembling, and massing erase that resistance, leaving gaps and low continuous terrain where distinct heights had stood. The `ط غ و` rock and fragment motifs have no available surface-root anchor.

#### Subchannel D. Barren vacancy after failed rain
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Rain misses or withdraws, growth fails, and place becomes empty, exposed, and lethal to a traveler.
- Active motifs: أرض مأفوكة صرف عنها المطر (`quranic:root_000041:B005/m01`); أرض أخطأها المطر (`quranic:root_000420:B003/m01`); أرض هلكين لا غيث فيها (`quranic:root_001596:B005/m01`); مهواة ومفازة مهلكة (`quranic:root_001596:B006/m01`); إخلاف النوء (`quranic:root_000450:B003/m01`); خواء الجوف (`quranic:root_000450:B005/m01`); قرع وانكشاف وخلو (`quranic:root_001219:B008/m01`); دوام الشيء وبقاؤه (`quranic:root_000142:B001/m01`); البقية وما يبقى من الشيء (`quranic:root_000142:B002/m01`); الإبقاء عفوا واستحياء (`quranic:root_000142:B003/m01`); حبس بعض الشيء وادخاره (`quranic:root_000142:B004/m01`); يبس الشجر (`quranic:root_000217:B008/m01`); ما بالدار نافخ ضرمة (`quranic:root_001529:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ف ك`: 69:9 (مُؤْتَفِكَٰتُ); `خ ط ء`: 69:9 (خَاطِئَةِ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ); `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ب ق ي`: 69:8 (بَاقِيَةٍ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ)
- Synthesis: A rainless, burned tract and a star-season that yields no water lead to hollow bellies, dead wood, stripped enclosures, an empty house, and a killing waste. Against that extinction, remaining, remnant, mercy, and saved reserve become sharply material counter-images: survival is what has not been stripped out of the place.

### 6. P1: Flood becomes conveyance and controlled water
- Semantic invariant: Water moves from excess to transport, capture, storage, and measured delivery.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:11 joins the overflowing water to human carriage in the vessel.
- Surprising reach: The flood scene opens into hydraulic engineering: channels, catch basins, wells, receiving hands, cloud-reservoirs, and reflective coatings.

#### Subchannel A. Torrent, vessel, and carried passengers
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An incoming mass of water drives a vessel that bears bodies and burdens through the flow.
- Active motifs: طغيان الماء وما يجري مجراه (`quranic:root_000936:B002/m01`); علو الماء والقوة الجارفة (`quranic:root_000937:B002/m01`); الماء المعروف وأصل اسمه (`quranic:root_001458:B001/m01`); ظهور الماء ودخوله وكثرته (`quranic:root_001458:B002/m01`); جريان الشيء وانسياحه (`quranic:root_000240:B001/m01`); إقلال المحمول الظاهر (`quranic:root_000357:B001/m01`); أداة الحمل ومركوبه (`quranic:root_000357:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ط غ ي`: 69:5 (طَّاغِيَةِ), 69:11 (طَغَا); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ج ر ي`: 69:11 (جَارِيَةِ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ)
- Synthesis: Overflowing water supplies the moving medium. Water enters and fills, the vessel runs, and a carrier or fitted transport frame keeps passengers and burdens above it.

#### Subchannel B. Catch basin, wellhead, and receiving vessel
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water is arrested in a depression, drawn or poured, and handed into a durable receptacle.
- Active motifs: موضع يمسك الماء (`quranic:root_000018:B006/m01`); مجتمع الماء في هبطة أو حول حصن (`quranic:root_000281:B003/m01`); الجِيأة مجتمع الماء (`quranic:root_000282:B002/m01`); وقائع الماء ومناقعه (`quranic:root_001675:B008/m01`); خشب البئر وموضع الساقي (`quranic:root_001000:B004/m01`); ماء رَبَب كثير (`quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`); تلقي الخارج إلى اليد (`quranic:root_001198:B007/m01`); سقي على أفواه الإبل (`quranic:root_001198:B014/m01`); إيصال الماء بالسقي والصب (`quranic:root_001458:B003/m01`); ضم الشيء في وعاء (`quranic:root_001664:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ج ي ء`: 69:9 (جَآءَ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ)
- Synthesis: Pool, fortress-side cistern, pond, timbered wellhead, abundant water, receiving assistant, and mouth-level watering form a complete collection system. Pouring moves the resource into a vessel, and the vessel's defining operation is to gather and keep what would otherwise continue downstream.

#### Subchannel C. Cloud reservoir and released rain
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water accumulates overhead, then breaks from the cloud in repeated or forceful release.
- Active motifs: رباب السحاب (`quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`); الحَمَل في السماء والسحاب (`quranic:root_000357:B010/m01`); أفاويق السحاب دفعات الماء (`quranic:root_001188:B008/m01`); انبعاق عزالي السحاب بالمطر (`quranic:root_001688:B003/m01`); وقوع المطر ومساقطه (`quranic:root_001675:B003/m01`); الريحان النبات والورق (`quranic:root_000609:B012/m01`); تفطر الشجر وطول النبات (`quranic:root_000609:B017/m01`); علو الشيء وربو الأرض (`quranic:root_001529:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `و ه ي`: 69:16 (وَاهِيَةٌ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ)
- Synthesis: Layered cloud and a dark water-bearing mass store the supply; cloud-drafts and burst rain release it. Falling water produces spring swelling, leafing, and plant height, so the same overhead reservoir that can overwhelm also renews the ground.

#### Subchannel D. Watered sheen, plating, and reflected appearance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A thin liquid-like surface changes how metal, face, fruit, or stone appears to an observer.
- Active motifs: كسوة المعدن بماء الذهب أو الفضة (`quranic:root_001458:B005/m01`); رونق كالماء في الوجه والكلام والثمر (`quranic:root_001458:B006/m01`); صفاء الماوية كالبلور والمرآة (`quranic:root_001458:B007/m01`); مرأى ومنظر ومرآة (`quranic:root_000531:B006/m01`); لمع البرق الخفي (`quranic:root_000428:B004/m01`); الصورة والهيئة المصورة (`quranic:root_000891:B003/m01`); العارض البادي من جهة (`quranic:root_001001:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `خ ف ي`: 69:18 (تَخْفَىٰ/خَافِيَةٌ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ)
- Synthesis: Metal plating can disguise its substrate, while watery brightness gives face, speech, fruit, crystal, and mirror their sheen. Reflection, faint lightning, formed image, and a distant appearing mass make appearance a surface event whose truth depends on what lies beneath.

### 7. P1: A message is sent, heard, and kept
- Semantic invariant: Communication is a custody chain in which a charge is dispatched, carried, received, retained, and made recallable.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:10 presents a messenger refused by his audience, and 69:12 turns the preserved event into a reminder held by a receptive ear.
- Surprising reach: The communication chain is materialized as herd after herd, flowing provision, a carried burden, an inward vessel, a legal deed, and an ear that acts as a gate of permission.

#### Subchannel A. Dispatch, carrier, and delivered charge
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A sender releases a carrier who bears words or responsibility toward a receiver.
- Active motifs: الإرسال والانبعاث (`quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`); الرسول والرسالة (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`); السير السهل واللين (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); الرفق والتؤدة (`quranic:root_000563:B004/m01`); التتابع والقطع (`quranic:root_000563:B005/m01`); المراسلة والمسايرة (`quranic:root_000563:B008/m01`); تسميات مفردة مخصوصة (`quranic:root_000563:B011/m01`); الجري الوكيل والرسول (`quranic:root_000240:B003/m01`); دوام الرزق والعطاء (`quranic:root_000240:B006/m01`); مجاراة الشيء والجري معه (`quranic:root_000240:B007/m01`); إقلال المحمول الظاهر (`quranic:root_000357:B001/m01`); تحمّل الأمانة والوزر (`quranic:root_000357:B003/m01`); الإتيان بالشيء واستحضاره (`quranic:root_000281:B004/m01`); تلقي الخارج إلى اليد (`quranic:root_001198:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ); `ج ر ي`: 69:11 (جَارِيَةِ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ج ي ء`: 69:9 (جَآءَ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ)
- Synthesis: Sending releases what had been held, and the messenger is the bearer of a statement. Easy gait, measured pace, successive groups, paired performance, and even a short projectile specify modes of transmission; agent, durable provision, and companioned movement keep the dispatch moving until a receiver takes it.

#### Subchannel B. Listening, permission, and inward retention
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The body opens an authorized point of reception and stores what enters as understood meaning.
- Active motifs: الأذن الجارحة وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000022:B001/m01`); الإصغاء وقبول المسموع (`quranic:root_000022:B002/m01`); العلم والإعلام بالنداء (`quranic:root_000022:B003/m01`); الإذن والترخيص بالأمر (`quranic:root_000022:B004/m01`); ضم المعنى في القلب (`quranic:root_001664:B001/m01`); ضم الشيء في وعاء (`quranic:root_001664:B002/m01`); انعدام المنصرف أو البد (`quranic:root_001664:B006/m01`); القائم على اليتيم (`quranic:root_001664:B007/m01`); استحضار الشيء بعد النسيان أو مع الحفظ (`quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`); الذكرى والتذكرة ما يذكّر (`quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`); الدراية والعلم (`quranic:root_000473:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ذ ن`: 69:12 (أُذُنٌ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `ذ ك ر`: 69:12 (تَذْكِرَةً); `د ر ي`: 69:3 (أَدْرَىٰ)
- Synthesis: The ear is both organ and attentive acceptance; announcement makes the content known, and permission governs entry. The heart then contains meaning as a vessel contains provisions, treats it as indispensable, and places it under care so that recollection can reactivate it.

#### Subchannel C. Deed, covenant, and protected remembrance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A socially binding memory is held in a document, pledge, or protected charge.
- Active motifs: ذكر الحق صك ووثيقة حق (`quranic:root_000516:B008/m01`); الذكرى والتذكرة ما يذكّر (`quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`); حقيقة يلزم حفظها (`quranic:root_000347:B007/m01`); ربابة تجمع القداح (`quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`); ربابة عهد وميثاق (`quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`); ضمان الشيء والتكفل به (`quranic:root_001198:B008/m01`); تحمّل الأمانة والوزر (`quranic:root_000357:B003/m01`); الحمالة والكفالة (`quranic:root_000357:B004/m01`); مِلاك الأمر وعِماده (`quranic:root_001444:B005/m01`); توقيع الكتاب وأثره (`quranic:root_001675:B011/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ذ ك ر`: 69:12 (تَذْكِرَةً); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `م ل ك`: 69:17 (مَلَكُ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)
- Synthesis: A deed of right, reminder, covenant, quiver-like repository, guarantee, carried trust, and written signature all preserve an obligation beyond the moment of speech. The affair has a structural mainstay, and failure is not mere forgetting but a breach in custody.

#### Subchannel D. Spoken name, reputation, and public recurrence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Repeated speech makes a person or event present in communal memory as name, fame, praise, or blame.
- Active motifs: جريان الذكر على اللسان (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`); ذكر المرء شرف وصيت (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`); الاسم تنويه ودلالة (`quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`); الصيت الحسن المنتشر (`quranic:root_000745:B008/m01`); رياء الناس (`quranic:root_000531:B005/m01`); الرسول والرسالة (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`); استقامة واعتدال واستواء (`quranic:root_001273:B008/m01`); ردع يقرع السامع (`quranic:root_001219:B006/m01`); الجَعْلة اسم مكان (`quranic:root_000248:B011/m01`); العلم بموضع أو يوم (`quranic:root_000450:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ذ ك ر`: 69:12 (تَذْكِرَةً); `س م و`: 69:16 (سَّمَآءُ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ)
- Synthesis: Tongued mention and carried message give a name public reach; favorable renown can rise, while place-name and named day stabilize reference. Performance for spectators and rebuke expose distorted social uses of visibility, and straight speech supplies the norm by which praise, blame, and reputation are heard.

### 8. P1: Falsehood redirects signs and surfaces
- Semantic invariant: Misrepresentation works by attributing what is not so, coating reality with a misleading surface, or turning perception away from its proper object.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:4 and 69:9-10 join denial, overturned settlements, sin, and refusal of the messenger.
- Surprising reach: Falsehood reaches bodily motion and materials: milk promises continuity then stops, an animal runs then checks, cloth lies by its colors, metal is plated, and a charm turns a face.

#### Subchannel A. False report, attribution, and inward self-deception
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A speaker or community asserts what is contrary to fact, assigns the lie to another, or houses deception in the self.
- Active motifs: خلاف الصدق (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); نسبة الشيء أو صاحبه إلى الكذب (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`); النفس الكذوب (`quranic:root_001290:B008/m01`); الإفك كذب مصروف عن الصدق (`quranic:root_000041:B002/m01`); عقل مأفوك وحيلة مصروفة (`quranic:root_000041:B006/m01`); ردع يقرع السامع (`quranic:root_001219:B006/m01`); رياء الناس (`quranic:root_000531:B005/m01`); الوقيعة في الناس (`quranic:root_001675:B013/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ); `ء ف ك`: 69:9 (مُؤْتَفِكَٰتُ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)
- Synthesis: Lie, accusation of lying, deceptive self, turned judgment, and backbiting form a single testimonial failure. Public performance seeks witnesses rather than truth, while rebuke presses the hearer to reverse direction and re-enter truthful judgment.

#### Subchannel B. Painted cloth, plated metal, and turning charm
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A fabricated outer appearance induces an observer or target to take one thing for another.
- Active motifs: الكذابة ثوب يكذب بحاله (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); كسوة المعدن بماء الذهب أو الفضة (`quranic:root_001458:B005/m01`); خرزة تقبل وجها إلى وجه (`quranic:root_001198:B016/m01`); رقية تمسك وتحبس (`quranic:root_000018:B004/m01`); مرأى ومنظر ومرآة (`quranic:root_000531:B006/m01`); رئي من الجن (`quranic:root_000531:B008/m01`); الصورة والهيئة المصورة (`quranic:root_000891:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ)
- Synthesis: Multicolored cloth 'lies' by its state, plating presents base metal as precious, and a bead or binding charm turns attention and action. Mirror-image and a presenting spirit supply receivers for that manufactured appearance; formed shape can be mentally real while materially misleading.

#### Subchannel C. Deflected current, missed rain, and diverted judgment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A current or faculty leaves its expected course, with barren land and weak counsel as outcomes.
- Active motifs: قلب الشيء وصرفه عن وجهته (`quranic:root_000041:B001/m01`); رياح مؤتفكات مختلفة المهاب (`quranic:root_000041:B004/m01`); أرض مأفوكة صرف عنها المطر (`quranic:root_000041:B005/m01`); عقل مأفوك وحيلة مصروفة (`quranic:root_000041:B006/m01`); مجاوزة الصواب وعدم إصابته (`quranic:root_000420:B001/m01`); أرض أخطأها المطر (`quranic:root_000420:B003/m01`); مواجهة الشيء للشيء (`quranic:root_001198:B001/m01`); ريح تقابل الدبور (`quranic:root_001198:B012/m01`); قصد الشيء واعتماده (`quranic:root_000473:B002/m01`); الإعراض تولية العرض (`quranic:root_001001:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ف ك`: 69:9 (مُؤْتَفِكَٰتُ); `خ ط ء`: 69:9 (خَاطِئَةِ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `د ر ي`: 69:3 (أَدْرَىٰ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ)
- Synthesis: Turning from truth shares a shape with crosswinds, rain diverted from a field, an action that misses its target, and a person who turns a side away. Facing and counter-wind make the lost orientation measurable, while a raid deliberately aimed at a place supplies the contrast between directed and deflected motion.

#### Subchannel D. Promised continuation that abruptly fails
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An action appears likely to persist but stops, delays, or proves unable to complete its course.
- Active motifs: ما كذب أن فعل أي ما لبث (`quranic:root_001290:B005/m01`); كذب لبن الناقة إذا ذهب ولم يدم (`quranic:root_001290:B006/m01`); كذب الوحشي إذا جرى ثم وقف (`quranic:root_001290:B007/m01`); سكوت لا يبدئ ولا يعيد (`quranic:root_001058:B003/m01`); ضعف وقصور عن الفعل (`quranic:root_000985:B001/m01`); فوت وسبق وإفلات من الطلب (`quranic:root_000985:B002/m01`); جمود ووقوف وكلال (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`); ترقب الشيء وانتظاره بالبصر (`quranic:root_000142:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ); `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ع ج ز`: 69:7 (أَعْجَازُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ب ق ي`: 69:8 (بَاقِيَةٍ)
- Synthesis: Immediate action can belie delay, milk can belie expected duration, and a running animal can halt to look back. Speechlessness, incapacity, escape, and a stalled mount turn failed continuation into a bodily scene; watchful waiting measures the gap between expectation and arrival.

### 9. P1: Sovereignty rests on raised supports
- Semantic invariant: Rule is sustained by a hierarchy of throne, upper position, structural mainstay, delegated care, and communal covenant.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:10 and 69:17 name the Lord, throne, angels, upper position, and eight bearers.
- Surprising reach: Royal elevation is tested through architecture and husbandry: a throne may be broken, a roof propped, a well timbered, a flock led, and a polity maintained by water and covenant.

#### Subchannel A. King, throne, and superior position
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A sovereign occupies a raised seat and exercises rule through rank, power, and recognized leadership.
- Active motifs: ربوبية وملك وسيادة (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); علم رباني (`quranic:root_000532:B003/m01`); رباني الملاحين (`quranic:root_000532:B017/m01`); المِلْك والتصرف (`quranic:root_001444:B002/m01`); المُلك والسلطان (`quranic:root_001444:B003/m01`); مِلاك الأمر وعِماده (`quranic:root_001444:B005/m01`); المتقدم القائد في الحيوان (`quranic:root_001444:B008/m01`); السرير المرتفع للملك (`quranic:root_001000:B001/m01`); قوام الأمر والعز إذا ثبت أو ثل (`quranic:root_001000:B006/m01`); علو المنزلة والفضل (`quranic:root_001188:B002/m01`); علو القهر والغلبة (`quranic:root_001188:B003/m01`); رعاية وحفظ وولاية (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); قوام وعماد ومعاش (`quranic:root_001273:B009/m01`); سادات كالجِبال (`quranic:root_000217:B012/m01`); مقروع مختار وخيار (`quranic:root_001219:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `م ل ك`: 69:17 (مَلَكُ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ)
- Synthesis: Lordship, learned divine formation, captaincy, ownership, kingship, and the affair's mainstay converge on the raised throne. Superior rank and coercive power explain the vertical relation, while recognized leaders and chosen chiefs translate it into the governed community; a 'broken throne' names the collapse of the whole arrangement.

#### Subchannel B. Canopy, scaffold, and load-bearing frame
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Raised members hold shelter, crop, rider, or burden in an ordered structure.
- Active motifs: السقف والمظلّة المرفوعة (`quranic:root_001000:B002/m01`); تعريش الكرم والعنب (`quranic:root_001000:B003/m01`); خشب البئر وموضع الساقي (`quranic:root_001000:B004/m01`); عريش المرأة على البعير (`quranic:root_001000:B005/m01`); ركوب الدابة واعتلاؤها (`quranic:root_001000:B011/m01`); الثبات في البلد (`quranic:root_001000:B012/m01`); آلة قائمة وجزء قائم (`quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`); علو الموضع والجهة (`quranic:root_001188:B001/m01`); مواضع علو أو شق في الجسد والآلة (`quranic:root_001188:B013/m01`); إقلال المحمول الظاهر (`quranic:root_000357:B001/m01`); أداة الحمل ومركوبه (`quranic:root_000357:B006/m01`); التحامل والمشقة (`quranic:root_000357:B007/m01`); تجمع مرتفع صلب (`quranic:root_000217:B001/m01`); أجزاء موصولة يقابل بعضها بعضا (`quranic:root_001198:B010/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ)
- Synthesis: Roof, vine-trellis, well frame, litter, mounted animal, and stable emplacement share the operation of bearing above a lower base. Standing machine parts, upper joints, transport fittings, and connected straps specify how the load is held; hardship appears when that frame must carry beyond easy capacity.

#### Subchannel C. Cultivation, custody, and delegated care
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A responsible agent maintains dependents, property, and resources until they reach a sound state.
- Active motifs: إصلاح وتربية وإتمام (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); ربيب وربيبة ورابة (`quranic:root_000532:B005/m01`); لزوم وإقامة ودوام (`quranic:root_000532:B007/m01`); شاة رُبّى وحداثة (`quranic:root_000532:B009/m01`); قوة الشيء وتماسكه (`quranic:root_001444:B001/m01`); مِلاك الأمر وعِماده (`quranic:root_001444:B005/m01`); الماء مَلَك الأمر (`quranic:root_001444:B007/m01`); رعاية وحفظ وولاية (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); نيابة وقيام مقام غيره (`quranic:root_001273:B007/m01`); الحمالة والكفالة (`quranic:root_000357:B004/m01`); الحميل المحمول في النسب (`quranic:root_000357:B005/m01`); الإبقاء عفوا واستحياء (`quranic:root_000142:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `م ل ك`: 69:17 (مَلَكُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ب ق ي`: 69:8 (بَاقِيَةٍ)
- Synthesis: Gradual upbringing, care for a stepchild, settled attendance, and a ewe kept near the house show rule as maintenance. Coherence, mainstay, water supply, substitution, guarantee, care for an unverified dependent, and merciful preservation all ask whether authority keeps what has been entrusted to it.

#### Subchannel D. Alliance, gathered people, and binding pledge
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Groups become a polity through assembly, recognized divisions, and a covenant that holds them together.
- Active motifs: ربة وجماعات كثيرة (`quranic:root_000532:B004/m01`); ربابة تجمع القداح (`quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`); ربابة عهد وميثاق (`quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`); رَبْرَب قطيع (`quranic:root_000532:B014/m01`); جماعة الناس والرجال (`quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`); مقام وإقامة في موضع (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); ضمان الشيء والتكفل به (`quranic:root_001198:B008/m01`); جماعة يقبل بعضها على بعض (`quranic:root_001198:B009/m01`); كثرة الرجال واجتماعهم (`quranic:root_001664:B008/m01`); كثرة كالجبل (`quranic:root_000217:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ)
- Synthesis: Large gatherings, lots held in one container, herds, councils, tribes, and crowds are not yet a polity until a pledge and guarantor bind their divisions. Place of assembly and sheer multitude provide the social material; covenant supplies the relation that makes it durable.

### 10. P1: Herd life follows generation, movement, and equipment
- Semantic invariant: Animal life is organized through mating, gestation, maturation, herd circulation, milking intervals, shelter, and predation.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:7's palm-trunk simile and the surah's transport and wind scenes activate extensive livestock imagery without narrating a husbandry episode.
- Surprising reach: The lexical network turns abstract completion and return into a full pastoral economy, from conception and birth through age, watering, tack, shelter, and the hunter's blind.

#### Subchannel A. Mating, conception, and approaching birth
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Male and female animals meet, conception becomes visible, and a carried young approaches emergence.
- Active motifs: ناقة بلغت حق الحمل والانتفاع (`quranic:root_000347:B008/m01`); تمام حال الحيوان وقوته (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`); أحق من الخيل يطابق خطوه أو يشتد بدنه (`quranic:root_000347:B014/m01`); ضراب الفحل وإنزاؤه (`quranic:root_001219:B003/m01`); ظهور حمل الناقة أو الشاة (`quranic:root_000531:B010/m01`); تحصن الفرس وصيرورته فحلا (`quranic:root_000609:B018/m01`); الحَمْل الباطن والثمر (`quranic:root_000357:B002/m01`); الحَمَل من الضأن (`quranic:root_000357:B009/m01`); ماء الفحل في الرحم (`quranic:root_001458:B004/m01`); اشتهاء الأنثى للفحل (`quranic:root_000248:B009/m01`); فرخ النعام (`quranic:root_000248:B010/m01`); الحامل تدنو ولادتها (`quranic:root_000548:B005/m01`); رفع الحمار رأسه عند الحمل (`quranic:root_001000:B009/m01`); تغذية ونشوء (`quranic:root_000537:B005/m01`); انتفاخ الشيء وامتلاؤه بالريح (`quranic:root_001529:B002/m01`); الطغيا الصغير من بقر الوحش (`quranic:root_000936:B009/m01`); تطاول الفحل على الشول (`quranic:root_000745:B003/m01`); إلقاء الثقل في المخالطة (`quranic:root_000482:B009/m01`); المواقعة في الجماع (`quranic:root_001675:B014/m01`); ترية الحيض (`quranic:root_000531:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `ر ج و`: 69:17 (أَرْجَآئِ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `س م و`: 69:16 (سَّمَآءُ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)
- Synthesis: Breeding readiness, mounting, deposited seed, visible pregnancy, inward burden, swelling, menstrual sign, and the nearness of birth form a continuous generational scene. A stallion rising over mares and bodily weight in intercourse make coupling explicit; young camel, lamb, ostrich chick, and wild-cattle calf mark successive products. The `ط غ و` calf motif has no available surface-root anchor.

#### Subchannel B. Age, bodily form, and usable maturity
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An animal passes from youth into a bodily state suited to work, breeding, or valuation.
- Active motifs: عود مسن فيه بقايا قوة (`quranic:root_001058:B008/m01`); كبر وقدم حتى يسمى عجوزا (`quranic:root_000985:B003/m01`); آخر الولد عند كبر الأبوين (`quranic:root_000985:B007/m01`); العُتِيّ من الكبر (`quranic:root_000981:B002/m01`); غلظ الخلقة والجسم (`quranic:root_000217:B003/m01`); خلقة مطبوعة (`quranic:root_000217:B004/m01`); قامة وقوام الجسم والطول (`quranic:root_001273:B011/m01`); السعة والانبساط الحسي (`quranic:root_000609:B009/m01`); ناقة بلغت حق الحمل والانتفاع (`quranic:root_000347:B008/m01`); تمام حال الحيوان وقوته (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`); أحق من الخيل يطابق خطوه أو يشتد بدنه (`quranic:root_000347:B014/m01`); صرصور البختي (`quranic:root_000857:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ع ج ز`: 69:7 (أَعْجَازُ); `ع ت و`: 69:6 (عَاتِيَةٍ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ)
- Synthesis: Old camel, late child, extreme age, thick bodily formation, inherited disposition, stature, breadth of foot, Bactrian camel, completed fatness, and matched stride describe maturity as a change in usable form. The same completion may qualify an animal for load, breeding, or price.

#### Subchannel C. Herd return, alternating groups, and milking rhythm
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Animals move out and back in ordered groups, pause at water, and yield milk on recurring intervals.
- Active motifs: رجوع بعد انصراف وتثنية بعد بدء (`quranic:root_001058:B001/m01`); عيادة ومعادة وزيارة راجعة (`quranic:root_001058:B005/m01`); الرواح والعشي ورد الماشية (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`); المراوحة والتناوب (`quranic:root_000609:B008/m01`); طرفا النهار المتعاقبان (`quranic:root_000859:B005/m01`); إبل تختلف ذهابا ومجيئا (`quranic:root_000859:B006/m01`); انتصاب وقيام بالبدن (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`); جمود ووقوف وكلال (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`); انتصاف النهار وقائم الظهيرة (`quranic:root_001273:B017/m01`); قوام في قوائم الشاة (`quranic:root_001273:B020/m01`); رجوع اللبن بين الحلبتين (`quranic:root_001188:B006/m01`); أخذ الشيء جرعات أو دفعات (`quranic:root_001188:B007/m01`); السير السهل واللين (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); التتابع والقطع (`quranic:root_000563:B005/m01`); اللبن والدر المتتابع (`quranic:root_000563:B006/m01`); أداة الحمل ومركوبه (`quranic:root_000357:B006/m01`); العدد سبعة وما يتفرع منه (`quranic:root_000669:B001/m01`); تكسّر التهالك في المشي والغنج (`quranic:root_001596:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ص ر ع`: 69:7 (صَرْعَىٰ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `س ب ع`: 69:7 (سَبْعَ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ)
- Synthesis: Return and repeated visitation become pastoral circulation: morning and evening groups cross, one herd arrives as another leaves, animals stand or tire, and the day reaches its central station. Milk returns between milkings and is taken in intervals; easy or swaying gait, successive droves, abundant yield, transport stock, and numbered watering cycles maintain the rhythm.

#### Subchannel D. Tack, restraint, shelter, and mounted frame
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Equipment connects animal body to load, rider, shelter, and restraint.
- Active motifs: آلة قائمة وجزء قائم (`quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`); قوام في قوائم الشاة (`quranic:root_001273:B020/m01`); السقف والمظلّة المرفوعة (`quranic:root_001000:B002/m01`); عريش المرأة على البعير (`quranic:root_001000:B005/m01`); منع الغنم من الرعي (`quranic:root_001000:B010/m01`); ركوب الدابة واعتلاؤها (`quranic:root_001000:B011/m01`); أداة الحمل ومركوبه (`quranic:root_000357:B006/m01`); التحامل والمشقة (`quranic:root_000357:B007/m01`); قبول الشيء برضا (`quranic:root_001198:B004/m01`); أجزاء موصولة يقابل بعضها بعضا (`quranic:root_001198:B010/m01`); إقبال العضو أو العلامة إلى جهة (`quranic:root_001198:B011/m01`); مقبض الشيء المأخوذ به (`quranic:root_000018:B012/m01`); فوق السهم موضع الوتر (`quranic:root_001188:B009/m01`); مواضع علو أو شق في الجسد والآلة (`quranic:root_001188:B013/m01`); جماعة النخل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000891:B005/m01`); خرقة إنزال القدر (`quranic:root_000248:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ)
- Synthesis: Standing hardware, diseased or constrained legs, livestock canopy, litter, grazing barrier, saddle or mount, load-bearing straps, udder cover, bridle parts, handle, notched fittings, and palm-shaped forelock create a detailed equipment scene. Even the heat-protective cloth belongs to this material practice of handling bodies and burdens.

#### Subchannel E. Predator, hunter's blind, and visible quarry
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hunter conceals approach, marks the target, and confronts predatory taking.
- Active motifs: السبع الحيوان المفترس (`quranic:root_000669:B002/m01`); الوقيعة والاغتياب كأذى السبع (`quranic:root_000669:B003/m01`); فعل سبعة: المبالغة في الأخذ والشر (`quranic:root_000669:B005/m01`); الختل والاستتار للصيد (`quranic:root_000473:B003/m01`); المدرى والحد المحدد (`quranic:root_000473:B004/m01`); رئي من الجن (`quranic:root_000531:B008/m01`); راية منصوبة (`quranic:root_000531:B011/m01`); الخروج للصيد (`quranic:root_000745:B006/m01`); قطيع البقر (`quranic:root_000891:B006/m01`); الطغيا الصغير من بقر الوحش (`quranic:root_000936:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ب ع`: 69:7 (سَبْعَ); `د ر ي`: 69:3 (أَدْرَىٰ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `س م و`: 69:16 (سَّمَآءُ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing)
- Synthesis: Beast, mauling insult, and exemplary ferocity establish predation. The hunter hides behind a mount, uses a pointed tool, follows a spirit-sign or raised standard, and goes out after quarry; a cattle herd and wild calf supply the visible target. The `ط غ و` calf remains lexically supported but surface-unanchored.

### 11. P1: Plants are raised, selected, and made valuable
- Semantic invariant: Cultivation converts water and soil into growth, then separates, selects, stores, and values the product.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:7's palm image supplies the direct plant anchor, while rain, earth, and remnant language materialize cultivation around it.
- Surprising reach: Palm trunks open onto seedbed, cloud, sap, sieve, jewelry, market, and reserved running strength rather than remaining a single botanical emblem.

#### Subchannel A. Watered swelling and vegetative renewal
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Moisture enters soil and plant, producing swelling, leafing, fruit, and sustained green matter.
- Active motifs: الأرض اللينة المنبتة (`quranic:root_000025:B002/m01`); زيادة وعلو (`quranic:root_000537:B001/m01`); تغذية ونشوء (`quranic:root_000537:B005/m01`); الريحان النبات والورق (`quranic:root_000609:B012/m01`); تفطر الشجر وطول النبات (`quranic:root_000609:B017/m01`); ظهور الماء ودخوله وكثرته (`quranic:root_001458:B002/m01`); إيصال الماء بالسقي والصب (`quranic:root_001458:B003/m01`); رونق كالماء في الوجه والكلام والثمر (`quranic:root_001458:B006/m01`); رباب السحاب (`quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`); ربة نبات (`quranic:root_000532:B012/m01`); الحَمْل الباطن والثمر (`quranic:root_000357:B002/m01`); الحَمَل في السماء والسحاب (`quranic:root_000357:B010/m01`); علو الشيء وربو الأرض (`quranic:root_001529:B003/m01`); يبس الشجر (`quranic:root_000217:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ)
- Synthesis: Soft fertile earth, rising growth, nourished young, leaves, lengthening shoots, water-filled fruit, rain cloud, persistent green plant, fruit burden, and spring swelling form one growth process. Dry wood marks its arrested state, while cloud and carried water explain the material source of renewal.

#### Subchannel B. Palm stand, shoots, and represented grove
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Palms appear as living tree, grouped grove, short shoot, decorative form, and named place.
- Active motifs: شجرة النخل (`quranic:root_001483:B001/m01`); حلي على صورة النخل (`quranic:root_001483:B005/m01`); أعلام المواضع والأشخاص (`quranic:root_001483:B006/m01`); جماعة النخل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000891:B005/m01`); النخل الصغار أو القصار (`quranic:root_000248:B006/m01`); السقوط والانهدام (`quranic:root_000450:B002/m01`); ربة نبات (`quranic:root_000532:B012/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ن خ ل`: 69:7 (نَخْلٍ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ)
- Synthesis: Tree and grove provide the direct image, but the palm is also reproduced as ornament, forelock-like form, short planting, and toponym. Uprooting reverses this multiplication, and the persistent green plant preserves the living form against the scene of collapse.

#### Subchannel C. Sifting, choosing, reserving, and pricing
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A harvest or stock is separated so that the best portion can be saved, displayed, and assigned value.
- Active motifs: تصفية الدقيق بالمنخل (`quranic:root_001483:B002/m01`); اختيار الأفضل (`quranic:root_001483:B003/m01`); تنخيل الثلج والودق (`quranic:root_001483:B004/m01`); مقروع مختار وخيار (`quranic:root_001219:B007/m01`); العوض والقيمة في البيع (`quranic:root_000206:B001/m01`); قيمة وتقويم وتسعير (`quranic:root_001273:B010/m01`); نفاق السوق (`quranic:root_001273:B018/m01`); حبس بعض الشيء وادخاره (`quranic:root_000142:B004/m01`); تمام حال الحيوان وقوته (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ن خ ل`: 69:7 (نَخْلٍ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ث م ن`: 69:7 (ثَمَٰنِيَةَ), 69:17 (ثَمَٰنِيَةٌ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ب ق ي`: 69:8 (بَاقِيَةٍ); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ)
- Synthesis: Sieving separates flour and bran; selection extracts the best; falling rain or snow is imagined through the same perforated motion. Choice stock, price, appraisal, active market, reserved running power, and finished fatness continue the operation from processing into exchange.

#### Subchannel D. Garden produce, insects, and damaged wood
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A cultivated plot contains edible growth alongside small creatures that inhabit or damage it.
- Active motifs: قرع اليقطين (`quranic:root_001219:B009/m01`); دويبة الجعلان (`quranic:root_000248:B008/m01`); دويبة الصرصر (`quranic:root_000857:B001/m01`); الأَرَضَة آكلة الخشب (`quranic:root_000025:B010/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ)
- Synthesis: Gourd, dung beetle, small chirping creature, and wood-eating termite create a compact garden ecology. The plant is not isolated produce: it shares soil and timber with organisms that consume, inhabit, or acoustically mark the plot.

### 12. P1: Time is counted through recurrence and completion
- Semantic invariant: Duration becomes intelligible through numbered units, returning cycles, and thresholds at which an action or state is complete.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:7 counts seven nights and eight days, 69:13-17 repeats singularity, that-day timing, and eight bearers.
- Surprising reach: Calendar units connect to lunar stations, milking intervals, noon balance, complete fatness, festival return, and the point at which delay expires.

#### Subchannel A. Numbered night, day, one, seven, and eight
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Discrete units partition a continuous duration and specify repeated or singular acts.
- Active motifs: العدد سبعة وما يتفرع منه (`quranic:root_000669:B001/m01`); الثمانية والجزء الثامن (`quranic:root_000206:B002/m01`); الانفراد والبينونة (`quranic:root_001631:B001/m01`); الواحد في العدد والآحاد (`quranic:root_001631:B002/m01`); اتحاد الأشياء في جهة واحدة (`quranic:root_001631:B005/m01`); إخراج الواحد أو جعله واحدا (`quranic:root_001631:B006/m01`); الليل خلاف النهار وظلمته (`quranic:root_001392:B001/m01`); مزاولة الأمر في الليل (`quranic:root_001392:B002/m01`); الليلة القريبة من اليوم (`quranic:root_001392:B003/m01`); التسمية بليلى (`quranic:root_001392:B004/m01`); وقت النهار المحدود (`quranic:root_001700:B001/m01`); مدة من الزمان (`quranic:root_001700:B002/m01`); كائنة اليوم وشدته (`quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ب ع`: 69:7 (سَبْعَ); `ث م ن`: 69:7 (ثَمَٰنِيَةَ), 69:17 (ثَمَٰنِيَةٌ); `و ح د`: 69:13 (وَٰحِدَةٌ), 69:14 (وَٰحِدَةً); `ل ي ل`: 69:7 (لَيَالٍ); `ي و م`: 69:7 (أَيَّامٍ)
- Synthesis: Seven, eight, one, night, and day supply explicit temporal units. Unity separates an act from repetitions yet can also join several things under one operation; nocturnal activity, the nearest night, a named night, ordinary daylight, extended duration, and a day-as-calamity thicken the count into lived time.

#### Subchannel B. Lunar, stellar, diurnal, and pastoral cycles
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Recurring natural positions regulate expectation, movement, and replenishment.
- Active motifs: أخذ القمر في منازله (`quranic:root_000018:B008/m01`); كواكب العرش (`quranic:root_001000:B008/m01`); انتصاف النهار وقائم الظهيرة (`quranic:root_001273:B017/m01`); الرواح والعشي ورد الماشية (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`); طرفا النهار المتعاقبان (`quranic:root_000859:B005/m01`); رجوع اللبن بين الحلبتين (`quranic:root_001188:B006/m01`); أخذ الشيء جرعات أو دفعات (`quranic:root_001188:B007/m01`); أفاويق السحاب دفعات الماء (`quranic:root_001188:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ص ر ع`: 69:7 (صَرْعَىٰ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ)
- Synthesis: The moon takes successive stations, constellations mark the sky, noon reaches balance, and evening initiates return. Morning and evening pair, milk and cloud-water replenish in intervals, and repeated small portions turn recurrence into a usable temporal measure.

#### Subchannel C. Threshold, termination, and completed state
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A process reaches the point where it becomes binding, finished, irreversible, or due.
- Active motifs: قطع حاسم (`quranic:root_000322:B001/m01`); تمام الزمن (`quranic:root_000482:B007/m01`); لزوم واجب واستحقاق ثابت (`quranic:root_000347:B002/m01`); تمام حال الحيوان وقوته (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`); تقدم الشيء أو إقباله (`quranic:root_001198:B002/m01`); ابتداء حاضر غير مهيأ (`quranic:root_001198:B015/m01`); إرجاء الشيء إلى وقت لاحق (`quranic:root_000548:B004/m01`); سقوط الشيء وثبوته (`quranic:root_001675:B001/m01`); العُتِيّ من الكبر (`quranic:root_000981:B002/m01`); العرض الطارئ الذي يعرض ثم يزول (`quranic:root_001001:B007/m01`); علو المقدار والحد (`quranic:root_001188:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح س م`: 69:7 (حُسُومًا); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ر ج و`: 69:17 (أَرْجَآئِ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ع ت و`: 69:6 (عَاتِيَةٍ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ)
- Synthesis: Decisive cutting, a complete span, binding entitlement, finished bodily condition, precedence, fresh beginning, postponement, fixed occurrence, passing condition, quantified upper limit, and terminal age all locate thresholds. The same logic explains why the event can be both awaited and, once arrived, no longer negotiable.

#### Subchannel D. Return, habit, visitation, and festival
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Repeated arrival turns isolated time into custom, social observance, and remembered return.
- Active motifs: رجوع بعد انصراف وتثنية بعد بدء (`quranic:root_001058:B001/m01`); مصير ومرجع ومعاد (`quranic:root_001058:B002/m01`); عادة ودرَبة ومواظبة (`quranic:root_001058:B004/m01`); عيادة ومعادة وزيارة راجعة (`quranic:root_001058:B005/m01`); عيد وحال يعاود (`quranic:root_001058:B007/m01`); مقام وإقامة في موضع (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); الرواح والعشي ورد الماشية (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ)
- Synthesis: Return after departure becomes destination, habit, sick-visit, recurring assembly, and festival. A place of staying and evening homecoming anchor recurrence spatially, so time is remembered by where bodies gather again.

#### Subchannel E. Expected resurrection and singular sovereignty
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Hope and expectation look toward the future rising in which singular rule is fully manifest.
- Active motifs: تطلع القلب إلى المأمول (`quranic:root_000548:B001/m01`); توقع الأمر ورمي الظن (`quranic:root_001675:B012/m01`); قيامة وبعث وقيام الساعة (`quranic:root_001273:B013/m01`); التفرد بالنظير والفضل (`quranic:root_001631:B003/m01`); وحدانية الله بلا شريك (`quranic:root_001631:B004/m01`); إما هلكت هلك على كل حال (`quranic:root_001596:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر ج و`: 69:17 (أَرْجَآئِ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `و ح د`: 69:13 (وَٰحِدَةٌ), 69:14 (وَٰحِدَةً); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ)
- Synthesis: Hope, expectation, resurrection, incomparability, divine unity, and the formula 'whatever the manner of death' gather around the coming event. Future time is not empty projection: it is oriented toward a rising governed by the one without partner.

### 13. P1: Passage is oriented by fronts, roads, and edges
- Semantic invariant: Movement depends on facing a destination, entering a route, negotiating boundaries, and finding a station or return point.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:9's arrivals, 69:11's vessel, 69:17's edges and above, and 69:18's presentation activate this spatial mechanism.
- Surprising reach: The route includes mountain tracks, fatal wastes, courtyard approaches, receiving points, sky margins, body sides, and a horse that cannot run straight.

#### Subchannel A. Facing, approach, and compelled arrival
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A mover turns a front toward a destination and is received, opposed, or forced into it.
- Active motifs: مواجهة الشيء للشيء (`quranic:root_001198:B001/m01`); تقدم الشيء أو إقباله (`quranic:root_001198:B002/m01`); جهة الشيء وعنده (`quranic:root_001198:B003/m01`); جهة الصلاة المتوجه إليها (`quranic:root_001198:B005/m01`); قبلة الفم والتقبيل (`quranic:root_001198:B006/m01`); طاقة على المقابلة (`quranic:root_001198:B013/m01`); ابتداء حاضر غير مهيأ (`quranic:root_001198:B015/m01`); المجيء والحصول (`quranic:root_000281:B001/m01`); المغالبة بكثرة المجيء (`quranic:root_000281:B002/m01`); الإتيان بالشيء واستحضاره (`quranic:root_000281:B004/m01`); الإلجاء والاضطرار (`quranic:root_000281:B005/m01`); المجيء والغلبة بالمجيء (`quranic:root_000282:B001/m01`); قصد الشيء واعتماده (`quranic:root_000473:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ج ي ء`: 69:9 (جَآءَ); `د ر ي`: 69:3 (أَدْرَىٰ)
- Synthesis: Front, precedence, source-side, ritual orientation, face-to-face contact, capacity to confront, and fresh approach map the destination. Arrival can be ordinary, repeatedly competitive, carry an object, or become coercive; deliberate targeting gives the route an agent and purpose.

#### Subchannel B. Old road, mountain track, and killing waste
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A traveler follows a known line through inhabited and dangerous terrain.
- Active motifs: قدم وطريق عود (`quranic:root_001058:B009/m01`); قارعة الطريق والدار (`quranic:root_001219:B010/m01`); مقام وإقامة في موضع (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); استقامة واعتدال واستواء (`quranic:root_001273:B008/m01`); العادة والطريقة الجارية (`quranic:root_000240:B002/m01`); السير السهل واللين (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); مهواة ومفازة مهلكة (`quranic:root_001596:B006/m01`); التهلك في المفازة تحيرا (`quranic:root_001596:B010/m01`); عروض ونواح وأسماء فنية أو موضعية (`quranic:root_001001:B014/m01`); دخول الجبال (`quranic:root_000217:B006/m01`); مصادفة رمل عريض (`quranic:root_000217:B011/m01`); حقحقة تجهد الظهر في السير (`quranic:root_000347:B012/m01`); ثقل يشق النفس (`quranic:root_000807:B003/m01`); الشقة البعيدة (`quranic:root_000807:B005/m01`); مَلَك الطريق والوادي (`quranic:root_001444:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ج ر ي`: 69:11 (جَارِيَةِ); `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ); `م ل ك`: 69:17 (مَلَكُ)
- Synthesis: Ancient road, exposed courtyard or thoroughfare, established way, personal route, easy gait, mountain entry, broad sand barrier, distant journey, road-center, and lethal wilderness make a continuous travel scene. Back-breaking pace and hardship test the traveler; straightness is the norm, while bewildered circling is its failure.

#### Subchannel C. Margin, side, upper edge, and meeting line
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Bounded things are located by side, rim, upper joint, and the line where two faces meet.

- Synthesis: Sky-rim, breadth-side, half, neck-side, upper joint, facing direction, linked parts, mouth corners, paired door leaves, matched counterparts, rope strands, and an affair's side describe boundaries through bodily and built analogies. These margins determine where parts meet, carriers stand, and presentation occurs.

#### Subchannel D. Station, dwelling, and return to place
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Motion resolves into standing, lodging, persistent residence, or a place to which one returns.
- Active motifs: انتصاب وقيام بالبدن (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`); مقام وإقامة في موضع (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); نيابة وقيام مقام غيره (`quranic:root_001273:B007/m01`); لزوم وإقامة ودوام (`quranic:root_000532:B007/m01`); الثبات في البلد (`quranic:root_001000:B012/m01`); لزوم الأرض والتثاقل إليها (`quranic:root_000025:B006/m01`); وقوع الطير وموضعه (`quranic:root_001675:B006/m01`); ربوض الدواب والإبل (`quranic:root_001675:B007/m01`); الرواح والعشي ورد الماشية (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ)
- Synthesis: Standing, residence, substitution in another's place, settled duration, fixed dwelling, adherence to the ground, a bird's landing, an animal's kneeling, and evening homecoming turn location into the outcome of movement.

#### Subchannel E. Interception, opposition, and sideways travel
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A route is crossed by an opposing body, causing exchange, blockage, or deviation from a straight course.
- Active motifs: الاعتراض حيلولة ومقابلة في الطريق (`quranic:root_001001:B004/m01`); المعارضة مقابلة ومماثلة ومبادلة (`quranic:root_001001:B006/m01`); العرضة نصب وقوة للتعرض (`quranic:root_001001:B011/m01`); السير عارضا وصعوبة الاستقامة (`quranic:root_001001:B012/m01`); التعرض والتصدي (`quranic:root_000025:B007/m01`); ميل عن القصد إلى الشقين (`quranic:root_000807:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ); `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ)
- Synthesis: Interposed barrier, matched opposition or exchange, an exposed target, sideways-running animal, deliberate confrontation, and deviation toward one side form one route-conflict scene. Movement remains intelligible by the straight line it fails to keep.

### 14. P1: Community is sustained by kin care and exchange
- Semantic invariant: Groups endure through lineage, guardianship, gift, valuation, labor, and the circulation of benefit.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:7's people, 69:10's messenger-community relation, and 69:12's gift of remembrance provide the social anchors.
- Surprising reach: The social body includes outsider, foundling, stepchild, elder, clan, guarantor, wage, tribute, market, and returning favor.

#### Subchannel A. Clan, tribe, crowd, and recognized elder
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Individuals are placed inside lineage and assembled groups with visible leaders.
- Active motifs: جماعة الناس والرجال (`quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`); مقام وإقامة في موضع (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); جماعة يقبل بعضها على بعض (`quranic:root_001198:B009/m01`); ربة وجماعات كثيرة (`quranic:root_000532:B004/m01`); رَبْرَب قطيع (`quranic:root_000532:B014/m01`); كثرة كالجبل (`quranic:root_000217:B002/m01`); سادات كالجِبال (`quranic:root_000217:B012/m01`); كثرة الرجال واجتماعهم (`quranic:root_001664:B008/m01`); الحميل المحمول في النسب (`quranic:root_000357:B005/m01`); آخر الولد عند كبر الأبوين (`quranic:root_000985:B007/m01`); عود مسن فيه بقايا قوة (`quranic:root_001058:B008/m01`); الخليق بالخير كالأرض الأريضة (`quranic:root_000025:B003/m01`); ابن الأرض الغريب (`quranic:root_000025:B004/m01`); أهل البيت من بني الأعمام (`quranic:root_000537:B007/m01`); عاد وأعلام منسوبة إليها (`quranic:root_001058:B012/m01`); نسبة الهالكي إلى الحداد (`quranic:root_001596:B007/m01`); انصداع الجماعة بالخلاف (`quranic:root_000807:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ع ج ز`: 69:7 (أَعْجَازُ); `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ); `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ)
- Synthesis: People, council, tribe, large assembly, herd, multitude, unverified child, late-born child, experienced elder, worthy member, earth-born stranger, cousins, tribal names, artisan lineage, and communal split make the community heterogeneous. Learned or leading 'mountains' supply authority, while outsiders, inherited labels, and schism test how belonging is recognized.

#### Subchannel B. Stepchild, foundling, guarantor, and merciful preservation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Care is extended to a dependent whose safety, lineage, or claim requires another to stand for them.
- Active motifs: إصلاح وتربية وإتمام (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); ربيب وربيبة ورابة (`quranic:root_000532:B005/m01`); شاة رُبّى وحداثة (`quranic:root_000532:B009/m01`); تلقي الخارج إلى اليد (`quranic:root_001198:B007/m01`); ضمان الشيء والتكفل به (`quranic:root_001198:B008/m01`); الحمالة والكفالة (`quranic:root_000357:B004/m01`); الحميل المحمول في النسب (`quranic:root_000357:B005/m01`); عيادة ومعادة وزيارة راجعة (`quranic:root_001058:B005/m01`); عائدة ومعروف يرجع (`quranic:root_001058:B006/m01`); رد الحق إلى صاحبه (`quranic:root_000609:B006/m01`); الإبقاء عفوا واستحياء (`quranic:root_000142:B003/m01`); القائم على اليتيم (`quranic:root_001664:B007/m01`); الفاقة حاجة وفقر (`quranic:root_001188:B011/m01`)

- Synthesis: Upbringing, stepchild, house-kept ewe, birth attendant, guarantor, indemnity, foundling, sick-visit, returning favor, restored right, mercy, poverty, and orphan guardian form a care network. The invariant is not sentiment alone but taking responsibility for another's continuity.

#### Subchannel C. Price, property, wage, and circulating benefit
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Goods and labor move through appraisal, appropriation, payment, profit, and gift.
- Active motifs: العوض والقيمة في البيع (`quranic:root_000206:B001/m01`); حق مخصوص يملكه صاحبه (`quranic:root_000347:B003/m01`); قيمة وتقويم وتسعير (`quranic:root_001273:B010/m01`); نفاق السوق (`quranic:root_001273:B018/m01`); عائدة ومعروف يرجع (`quranic:root_001058:B006/m01`); أجر مجعول على عمل (`quranic:root_000248:B005/m01`); أرض مأخوذة للنفس (`quranic:root_000018:B005/m01`); الاتخاذ والاكتساب (`quranic:root_000018:B010/m01`); المِلْك والتصرف (`quranic:root_001444:B002/m01`); زيادة الربا في المعاملة (`quranic:root_000537:B003/m01`); اقتراع ومساهمة (`quranic:root_001219:B004/m01`); وزن سواء ومقدار معتدل (`quranic:root_001273:B015/m01`); العرض متاع وبدل وحظ من الدنيا (`quranic:root_001001:B008/m01`); الراح الخمر (`quranic:root_000609:B015/m01`)

- Synthesis: Price and owned right support appraisal; a thriving market, returning benefit, work-fee, appropriated estate, acquisition, ownership, lot-drawing, equal weight, worldly goods, wine as commodity, and interest-bearing increase specify different transfers. The scene asks whether value circulates as fair compensation, private taking, chance allocation, or exploitative surplus.

#### Subchannel D. Compelled labor and willing generosity
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Work may be extracted under force or offered through ease, honor, and active care.
- Active motifs: تسخير العامل بلا أجرة (`quranic:root_000685:B002/m01`); الرخاء وطيب الإعطاء (`quranic:root_000563:B010/m01`); الأريحية والنشاط للمعروف (`quranic:root_000609:B010/m01`); عزم ونهوض إلى الأمر (`quranic:root_001273:B003/m01`); رعاية وحفظ وولاية (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); إصلاح وتربية وإتمام (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); أجر مجعول على عمل (`quranic:root_000248:B005/m01`)
